LOW — Card Archive

In The End

11 June 2003
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Long Live the Legacy :: Before the show, Jason Warrior arrives backstage. He has a confident look on his face as he walks into his dressing room. The camera rolls as he steps inside and fumbles with a light switch. :: Jason Warrior: What the hell? What's wrong with the lights? :: A thud is heard and some blood splatters out into the hallway. We hear a few more bumps and thuds and sounds of fighting continues. After a couple moments Legacy steps out of the dressing room and looks to the camera. :: Legacy: My chance to join Lights Out Wrestling is on the line tonight and he's one of the ones who stands in the way. If anyone thinks the match tonight is going to end with a new Aftermath Champion they are dead wrong. I am going to see to it personally that only one man stands victorious tonight and his name is Legacy! I'll see you in hell Jason Warrior. In other words, Try to make it to the match, because that was just the beginning! :: Legacy walks away holding his bat and turns the corner out of the view of the camera as Jason Warrior crawls out of his dressing room, very disoriented bleeding from a gash in his head. He slams his fist into the floor and stares at Legacy turning the corner. :: Jason Warrior: It's not over Legacy! It's not over! The Aftermath Championship is going around MY WAIST! You sick freak! You'll never make it in Lights Out Wrestling! :: Jason Warrior coughs up a little blood and then collapses as several other wrestlers rush over to help him up. :: Lights On On June 11th, 2002, a dream was born. And as simple as that, the dream turned into a nightmare, at the hands of one man. Mike Meyhu. The Number One Brawler in the Business new the weaknesses of his enemy, Trigger, and he knew that at the hands of false trust, he could attain victory. To him, it meant that he could attain revenge. And so he did it. He won Trigger's trust. And at his most vulnerable moment, he revealed his true colors. The effect was greater than what he could have imagined. He didn't just crush Trigger. He destroyed Trigger. The Infinite Warrior knew that he was facing defeat, and knew that it was nobody's fault but his. Therefore, he stepped down from his pedestal, and introduced the next generation of Warrior, Tristan Belmont, who would lead Lights Out Wrestling into the next generation of the battle. On June 11th, 2002, a dream was born. And on June 11th, 2003, there is a risk that it will die. And so the original Warrior will return to the ring for one final match. It will determine who shall stay, and who shall go. Trigger will fight against Mike Meyhu... And good or bad, the war will end tonight. :: As the opening music to our song plays softly, we are recapped of memories we must keep with us. Some memories we would rather forget. :: Arf arf Yeah, yeah, yeah (Grrrr) Uh, Yeah don't get it twisted This rap shit, is MINE! :: We head to the backstage area into the World Champion's dressing room. Their stands Tristan Belmont facing back first to the camera looking at the World Title. As the camera turns slightly Tristan's face can be seen from a corner view. The crowd is shocked at what they see. :: Casey, that's not Belmont! That's… It's JESSE JAMESTER! :: Jesse has on a shirt that has "Warrior" written on it. Their body builds are almost exact, so only to the faces are they different. Jesse holds the world title in his hands as though it is his. The gold shines in his eyes just like a child stares at candy. :: Jamester: My belt… My belt… MY BELT!!! :: At those words Tristan Belmont appears from the shower wearing a towel. :: Tristan: WHAT THE!? Jamester: IT'S MINE! Tristan: The belt means nothing Jamester… Do you not realize that? I am the champion, and for that single reason I could careless if you take the belt. :: Jamester looks down. Everything Tristan says makes sense. The World title belt itself does not make the champion, the person who defeats who holds the belt itself is the champion. :: Jamester: You may not understand me Belmont, but one thing you should know is I am not a bad guy. Sure I enjoy hurting you, but why, because you're the main man, you're the champion! Everyone wants a piece of you, but I want a bigger piece… Why do you think I threatened Trigger's life? I wanted to see how much you cared for the belt and unlike me you hold it. Something I have worked for since I came here. I can feel the belt within my grasp but it keeps slipping. Now I have a firmer grasp and it will not fall! Tristan: Good, then when we fight… Let it be a fight to the end. Jamester: So let it be… Motherfucker, it's NOT... A... GAME! Fuck what you heard It's what you hearin :: "Droppin' Plates" by Disturbed hits, and out comes 'Mean' Mike Meyhu. :: Oh no. Ooooh no. What the hell is he doing out here? Let me guess, now he lost his job? Casey, this is his job... He's the boss! *Shrug* Apparently he's got some real bad luck. :: He marches down the rampway, and slides into the ring, pushing aside the referee who attempts to clean up the scene. Now the Most Powerful Man in the Business signals to the ring announcer for a microphone, and moments later holds it before his lips. :: Mike Meyhu: Trigger, Trigger, Trigger. Why is it that all I hear about is that Infinite Warrior? That undeserving, dispicable thing? That thing that ruins an innocent man's life, only to make it seem as if he is slaying a dragon? Trigger didn't do anything special. Trigger just ruined me. You don't care, do you? :: He turns his attention to the fans, who hate him more with every word he spews. :: Mike Meyhu: No, of course you don't. Because you're too wrapped up in the reality that he shows you. You're too stuck on the concept that everything he does is right, and everything I do is wrong. Well ya know what? You're wrong! You're wrong. :: More boos. They are throwing trash into the ring now. :: Give me a break! What's this guy trying to pull here? :: After a long pause, Mean Mike goes on. :: Mike Meyhu: He calls himself a Warrior. What the hell is that supposed to mean? That he's brave? That he's strong? That he's courageous? Because he's not. He's none of those things. He hides in the shadows, crying to himself. He suffers blow after blow, and cannot even return words. He hasn't the courage to even step out into the arena, to show himself to his worshippers. :: The crowd is in a rage now, filling the air with their shouts, blocking out even the deep voice of the former World Champion, who without much hesitation moves on. :: Mike Meyhu: Well if he's a Warrior, then I want to show you all that a Warrior can crumble. That's right-- I want to destroy Trigger, with my hands. I want to make him bleed. I want to hear his bones break. Only then will my trauma be ended. Only when his trauma begins. I don't like where this is headed, Casey. I don't like where this is headed at all. Mike Meyhu: A Warrior match, that's what it will be. Let's see who the real Warrior is. I don't think we'll have to wait until the Pay Per View. I don't think Trigger has the courage to answer my challenge. :: A sudden shock rings out. Is he really serious? Is he really challenging the Infinite Warrior to a wrestling match? :: Good lord! He can't be serious! :: Like an answer to our prayers, "Dead On Arrival" by X-Raided hits, and Trigger rushes out from the back, dressed in tank top and jeans, carrying a microphone. The fans are estatic as he begins to speak. :: TRIGGER IS HERE! TRIGGER IS IN THE ARENA! Yeah, but how is he going to answer to that? Trigger: Here I am, Meyhu. Talking to you. Not to the fans, not to myself, but to you. I'm done with this, Meyhu. I am sick of being pushed around, trampled on, and destroyed. I'm sick of you, Meyhu. I'm sick of fearing you. :: The fans are solemn at such an ugly truth. :: Trigger: No more. Trigger: No longer will I fear you, Meyhu. No longer will you control my emotions, pulling me in directions like a puppeteer. I WON'T LET YOU! :: Now they cheer for the emphatic statement. :: Good for him, good for Trigger! He knows what's wrong, and he knows how to fix it. Trigger: It's been too long. Much too long. I started Lights Out Wrestling, because I wanted a change. I was done with the politics, with the egos, with the terror. Wrestling. That's all I wanted. Wrestling. You're right, Mike Meyhu. You made this company. But you made it into something dirty. And it is my duty to the fans that keep this dream alive, to the wrestlers that put their hearts into this business, to clean it up. I am telling you that I will clean it up. Not maybe. Not possibly. I will. Or I will die trying. :: More cheering erupts, or simply intensifies, as it never really stopped. :: Now this is the Trigger that we all know. This is the Trigger that built this company. Nobody can oppose him, nobody even tries. Trigger: In a little over a week, an event will arise, known as In The End. That symbolizes a lot to me, Meyhu. You see, exactly one year before that date, Lights Out Wrestling was born. I had a dream, and I wanted to make it a reality. It wasn't about greed, power, or even revenge. It was about this dream, and this was a dream that I set out to build. One year ago, I created Lights Out Wrestling. And one year later, it is in your command. Trigger: How did this happen? How did my dream turn into a nightmare? It was all you. You blame me for your problems, when it is you who ruined this for me. You who ruined this for everyone. Trigger: No more. Trigger: No longer will I let you ruin this. No longer will I let you destroy me from the inside. No longer will I let you destroy my dream. No longer will I let you destroy Lights Out Wrestling. I WON'T LET YOU! :: He sounds almost triumphant at the statement, and the crowd applauds. Mike Meyhu stares onward, fury boiling in his face. Now, however, the Infinite Warrior turns serious. :: Trigger: At In The End, I will take up your challenge. But it will not be just a Warrior match. It will be the last. Between you and I, at least, Meyhu, because in the end, only one force can remain. In the end, it will be either you or me. The victor stands tall and proud, while the loser must lose this battle once and for all. The loser must leave for all eternity. Trigger: Because in the end, Mike Meyhu, hatred never dies. :: And though his expression remains the same, there is a look of understanding in the eyes of Mean Mike Meyhu... For he knows that the saying is true. :: It's what you hearin (Listen!) It's what you hearin (Listen!) It's what you hearin (Listen!) “ All of this comes to an end soon “ … “ All of it “ Who’s talking? :: The scene opens up to Tristan Belmont sitting in the middle of his locker room, head in hands. He does not look at us whatsoever, he is too focused :: “ Last stop before the End. Evil and Good clash at the PPV. One year ago, I stepped into LOW. One year ago … The End approaches. My time here is not over, but someone’s is. Jesse, you asked for this, and forgive me Trigger, forgive me for the breach of rule I am about to do, but Jesse Jamester, setting you straight will be a fun moment I have yet to have had in my life. I will hurt you “ :: Belmont raises his attention up to the camera now :: “ To you it’s about the World Title, but to me, the World Title means nothing anymore! You have soiled it! You see it as an object; I saw it as a stepping-stone, as a moment of pride. You want it for the fame, for the attention, while I want it for LOW. I want it so that my side, the side of right stands tall. You could not care less about LOW; you want your own personal fame. To let you take it is to make sure that LOW dies, and I will not allow it … “ :: He brings his attention back into his hands, and lets his head crash into them:: “ I will not allow it “ ........................................................................ X gon' give it to ya Fuck wait for you to get it on your own X gon' deliver to ya Knock knock, open up the door, it's real With the non-stop pop pop and stainless steel Go hard gettin busy with it But I got such a good heart I'll make a motherfucker wonder if he did it Damn right and I'll do it again Cuz I am right, so I gots to win! :: The lights flash on, and suddenly the dark empty space is filled with lively excitement. Tens of thousands in attendence, here with a first hand view of what will go down in history as one of the most spectacular events recorded. Spectacular in its entirety. Spectacular beyond belief. :: Break bread with the enemy But no matter how many cats I break bread with I'll break who you sendin me You motherfucker never wanted nothin But your wife said, that's for the light day I'm gettin down, down Make it say freeze But won't be the one endin up on his knees (Whoo) Please, If the only thing you cats did is come out to play Get out my way First we gonna ROCK! Then we gonna ROLL! Then we let it POP! GO LET IT GO! :: They know that the outcome may not be to their liking. It will surely change what they know, and what they are familiar with, but nevertheless they must risk disappointment for a glimpse of success: peace. No more violence. No more hatred. It is possible. But will it happen? If it does, it will happen tonight. :: Ain't never gave nothin to me But everytime I turn around Cats got they hands out wantin something from me I ain't got it so you can't get it Lets leave it at that cuz I ain't wit it Hit it wit full strength I'm a jail nigga So I face the world like it's Earl in the bullpen You against me, me against you Whatever, whenever What the fuck you gon do? I'm a wolf in sheep's clothing Only nigga that you know that can chill Come back and get the streets open I've been doing this for nineteen years Wanna fight me? Fight these tears I put in work and it's all for the kids But these cats done forgot what work is (UH-HUH!) They don't know who we be Lookin! But they don't know who they see First we gonna ROCK! Then we gonna ROLL! Then we let it POP! GO LET IT GO! :: It all comes down to this. One night. One night that will burn in our memories for years. Decades. It all comes down to this. We are ready. Are you? :: Fans! Welcome... To... In The End! God, did you ever think we'd make it this far? I'm Jerry Donalo, along with Casey Sanders, and to answer your question Casey, I sure as hell did. Countless people have tried to destroy us, but we just keep coming back, bigger and badder. And look at the show we've got for you tonight! Now whoever booked this thing has got to be a genious. They sure must be an evil genius... Tonight's final of three main events, which sees Mike Meyhu defending his ownership and his career against original owner, Trigger... That's unevenly matched, and I think it's going to lead to nothing but disastor. Jerry, it is something that absolutely needs to be done. Mike Meyhu has tried to kill Trigger numerous times, but never with his own hands. Tonight, that's what he's planning on doing, and the only way for the Infinite Warrior to overcome his past is to face it head on. It's going to be a very anxious competition... As will the sub main event, which puts World Champion Tristan Belmont against challenger Jesse Jamester. Now, Jamester was the first man to sign a LOW contract; but Belmont was on the first show as was he. This time, Jamester's not the veteran taking on the newcomer-- this time, it's all about the gold. There's emotions involved as well, however, there's gotta be. I don't know if there could possibly be any more emotions involved than the first main event, where former partners Jason Kage and Eric Russo go head to head. There is only one word to describe what that match is going to be: vicious. Definitely! Oh, and by the way, we can't forget that the Xtreme Championship is on the line where former champion Judgement gets his revenge on Reality/Mayhem. They both want this really bad... And if I know Judgement, I know he's going to go to any lengths to get that gold back around his waist. Not to mention the fact that we get to see "Nightmare" Sean Dahmer finally confronting his mysterious attacker, Scorpion. That should be one for the ages right there. Scorpion is a former star of CCW and PCW... His presence in LOW is sure going to heat things up. I think it already has! But if you're looking for two real veterans of LOW, then you have to find Iron Lung taking on Matt Robinson. Lung relinquished the Aftermath Championship last week, and I have a feeling Robinson is going to be pretty pissed if he takes the victory and doesn't end up with any gold. We'll just have to wait and see how things unfold. In a competition where we will see two future main eventers, there's Big Daveylicious and Annihilation. Both have what it takes to go far in Lights Out Wrestling... But they're going to have to push each other out of the picture if they want to be noticed individually. One match I'll be particularly looking forward to is the one involving Canyon Thunders and Cyrus Skar-- These two are just waiting to break out into superstardom, and tonight could be the very night that it finally happens. Either of them could be a future World Champion... But which will it be? And of course, there's the Tag Team Championship match. Both teams made it through that grueling tournament, and now they are reaping the rewards as four men get their first opportunity at gold in Lights Out Wrestling. Will it be Phoenix Rose and Derek Vinyard, or will it be Josh Starwind and The Blue Coral? Fans, this is going to be a remarkable, memorable night, and so sit tight because in a few moments we will witness the first ever Battle Cry match, where four LOW stars will get the chance to take home the Aftermath Championship, and four free agents will get the chance to sign a LOW contract. Don't go away!!! :: With that, our scene fades-- our hearts beating faster by the second. :: The Arrival :: The tall figure of Judgement appears in the back of the arena, as he gets out of his limo. Along are his band mates who he has gotten ringside tickets to. The get their bags and then head to Judgement's locker room. Right before they enter Judgement's locker room the reporter Jeff Leon comes up to Judgement to ask him a few questions. :: Leon: So Judgement how do you feel about tonight? Judgement: I am feeling great man, lots of positive thinking, and I am focused on winning that title! Leon: Are we going to see a lot of extreme wrestling? Judgement: Of course, I am the King of Extreme always remember that! I might of lost the title to Reality/Mayhem a while back, but right now I am more focused than ever before, and I can already feel that title in my hands. junkyard or ring, I don't care, I will fight anyplace, anytime, anybody! Leon: So are you saying that you don't see Reality/Mayhem as a threat at all? Judgement: That is not what I am saying at all! You have to open your ears every once in a while and listen. I know that kid has lots of talent, it is just the last time I stepped in the ring with him to defend the Xtreme Championship I was underestimating him, now I know him a lot better and I am fully prepared for this one. It will be one hell of a battle, just the better man will come out on top, and that is me! Leon: May I ask why your friends are here tonight? Are they going to do anything with your match? Judgement: Are you trying to say that I am going to cheat to win my match? Nah man calm down a little bit, just playing around. I bought them front row tickets to go see the show and that is what they are here for, and that is all they will be doing. Nothing more, nothing less! Leon: There are rumors going around that you are going to be speaking your mind later tonight, what could that be about? Judgement: That is correct, I have to apologize to my fans publicly and I will do that right before I head out to my battle at the Junkyard. :: With that, the scene fades. :: An Unusual Greeting :: We're taken to the backstage area where we come across Jason Warrior's locker room. Inside, we can see the Warrior, still bleeding from the earlier attack, but nevertheless preparing for tonight's Battle Cry match. His face is dawned with red and black face paint and his eyes speak determination for themselves. He heads over to the mirror and looks at himself; he thinks of everything he's stood for in his life. He knows much is writing on tonight's event and that thought sends butterflies right to his stomach. His adrenaline is pumping and his mind clear; he is now ready for the End that will usher in a new beginning for him. Before heading out to the arena, a Lights Out Wrestling backstage crew member knocks on his door. :: "Mr. Warrior, I've got a letter here for ya." :: A letter? he thinks to himself. Jason has no idea who it could be from; hopefully Alla or maybe even Tyron. He answers the door and takes the letter, sealed in an envelope, from the short pudgy women. He smiles and thanks her for the unexpected delivery. :: "Thank you...he looks at her name tag...Janet." "Good luck tonight Jason." :: She smiles and continuos on with her business, letting Jason be in his locker room. Jason takes a seat and notices that there is no writing on the envelope except for that of his name, "Jason Warrior." He rips open the envelope and unfolds the small white stationary paper. It reads... Break a leg brother...I'll be watching. ~Drake Jason's eyes widen as amazement consumes his mind. He's surprised to hear from his brother on such short notice; maybe this is his way of saying the past is in the past, and its time for a new beginning. Jason places the note in his bag and feels even more confident than he did before. He is now ready. For tonight is the End of the old...A the beginning of a new. :: Battle Cry Match The opening match tonight is the BattleCry match where four LOW up and comers will take on four free agents. The stipulations: If the winner already competes in Lights Out Wrestling he will become the Aftermath champion. If the winner is a free agent he will earn an LOW contract. The stakes are high in this match, only the strongest will prevail. But is the strongest even on our roster? I guess we'll find out now. Announcer: Ladies and Gentlemen, the following match will be a BattleCry match up. Two teams of four have been decided, four of those members free agents and four of those members LOW stars. One man from each team will begin and every two minutes a random member from either team will enter the match until all eight men are in the ring. However only one man can win the match. Entering first, from Lights Out Wrestling, Canadian Perfect, CHRIS WRESTLING! ::Chris Wrestling appears onstage to a pop from the fans.:: Canadian Perfect! One of the Aftermath divisions biggest stars is making his way to the ring to start this match. Starting this match isn't really an advantage though, it means you have to outlast everyone else, all of whom enter after you. Announcer: And his opponent, free agent.. JOE COOOOOOOL!!! ::The free agent makes his way onto the stage and starts confidently down the entrance ramp to the ring.:: This young man has a huge opportunity in front of him, he could win a contract with Lights Out Wrestling tonight! But he's in the same boat as Chris Wrestling, he is one of the first entrants... he has his work cut out for tonight. ::Joe Cool steps into the ring and stares across to the turnbuckle opposite the one he stands in at his opponent, Canadian Perfect. The bell rings and both men step forward to the center of the ring and start circling. Joe Cool lunges in and locks up with Canadian Perfect but they break and continue to circle each other, waiting for the perfect moment to strike. The lock up once more and Canadian Perfect starts forcing Joe Cool back into a turnbuckle but they break and Chris Wrestling, a slightly aggravated look on his face, starts looking again for the time to strike.:: You can feel the tension between these two men... any minute now... ::Chris Wrestling slowly backs away from Joe Cool who quickly gets away from the corner and then suddenly charges Canadian Perfect with a clothesline. Canadian Perfect ducks just in time and turns to face Joe Cool, who also turns, not letting his back be exposed to Chris Wrestling to capitalize on. Chris Wresting suddenly darts in though, catching Joe Cool off guard. He grabs Joe Cool around the waist and lifts him up and spine busters him to the mat. The crowd cheers as Chris Wrestling stands above a downed Joe Cool momentarily before jumping on him and throwing some punches into his fortunately guarded face. Joe Cool bucks up with his hip and throws Chris Wrestling forward, Canadian Perfect catches himself with his arms and Joe Cool flips him off with his knees to Wrestling's chest in monkey flip like fashion.:: Hey, this guys got some moves, I think he took Chris Wrestling by surprise there! ::Canadian Perfect gets to his feet where Joe Cool is waiting. They tie up and Cool thrusts a hard knee into Canadian Perfect's side. Chris Wrestling collapses to the mat just as Jason Warrior appears on the stage. He runs down the ramp and slides into the ring and dives at Joe Cool, fists flying. The two fall to the mat throwing techniques reminiscent of a high school brawl. Chris Wrestling crawls to a turnbuckle and starts to get himself up as Joe Cool and Jason Warrior break for a moment. Joe Cool swings a wide right hand and Jason Warrior blocks and whips Joe Cool into the ropes, Cool ricocheting off and coming back at Warrior who ducks to avoid a clothesline and then catches Cool with a neckbreaker as he runs past.:: Jason Warrior with a neckbreaker on Joe Cool! Warrior is fresh, he's got the advantage over this free agent, plus right now technically its two on one, two LOW members against Joe Cool. ::Joe Cool gets to his feet and is hit with a right hand from Chris Wrestling, and then is hit with a forearm from behind from Jason Warrior. Joe Cool stumbles forward into the hands of Chris Wrestling who lifts him up and bodyslams him to the mat. Canadian Perfect follows up with a leg drop and Jason Warrior elbow drops him. Joe Cool rolls out of the ring and the LOW members follow. The come up on either side of the recovering Cool and grab him by the back of the head. Joe Cool's head is forced into the fan guardrail once.. twice.. three times, and then Cool collapses to the floor. The LOW members lift him up and suplex him to the floor.:: Jason Warrior and Chris Wrestling using their two on one advantage to take out Joe Cool. Good strategy too, every guy that gets taken out gets you closer to winning that Aftermath title or contract. ::Joe Cool starts to crawl away but Canadian Perfect and Jason Warrior each grab one of Cool's ankles and lift them into the air and then hurl them back down, Joe Cool's knees take most of the damage. Wrestling and Warrior then waste no time in laying into the downed free agent with their boots as Cool tries to cover himself from all the shots he can. The LOW members seem to tire of stomping him and they grab him and throw him back into the ring where Cool gets up with the help of the ropes and stands ready to fight.:: Joe Cool showing a lot of perseverance, a lot of toughness. He's going to need that if he wants to get into LOW. ::Warrior and Wrestling re-enter the ring and run toward Joe Cool as the two minute time limit is up. Joe Cool charges the two LOW team members and clotheslines them both to the mat and Cool to his knees. He gets back to his feet but is blindsided by Joseph Magnus, the next entrant into the match. Magnus hits Cool from behind with his forearm and then German suplexs him. Cool lands on his head and again rolls out of the ring.:: It's now THREE ON ONE! Three LOW members are in the ring against one member of the free agent team! Hey, its random, anything can happen. Joe Cool just got REALLY unlucky! ::Warrior and Wrestling slowly recover as Joseph Magnus quickly exits the ring to continue his assault on Joe Cool, who tries to get away from him but Magnus catches up quickly and grabs Cool around the back of the neck and throws him back into the ring where Warrior and Wrestling stand waiting to prey on the hurt Cool. Cool gets up facing them and starts to back up to the ropes but Magnus hits him in the back and forces him forward to where Warrior and Wrestling wait in front of him. They pick him up and throw him up into the air and a flapjack like move, and he comes down hard on his chest and face. Magnus slides back into the ring and the three LOW members start stomping the life out of Cool.:: Joe Cool is in trouble now! ::Warrior, Wrestling, and Magnus continue to stomp Cool when suddenly out of the crowd comes Mike Marvelous, a member of the free agent team, slides into the ring with a chair.:: WHAT THE!? I DON'T THINK THE TWO MINUTE LIMIT IS UP YET! ::Marvelous slides into the ring and smashes the chair into the back of Joseph Magnus' head. He turns and hits Chris Wrestling in the forehead, and then rams the chair into Jason Warrior's midsection, doubling him over. Magnus and Wrestling roll out of the ring to recover as Marvelous throws the chair aside and grabs Warrior's head and DDTs him. Warrior gets up quickly, somehow recovered from the chair shot to his gut but Marvelous kicks him in the stomach and piledrives him. Joseph Magnus, partially recovered, slides into the ring but meets a fist to the face that sends him back to the floor outside the ring. Jason Warrior starts to get up just as the two minute timer is up, and ducks a punch from Marvelous as Legacy, free agent, enters the ring.:: Well now its three on three I guess, although Mike Marvelous came in from nowhere before his time was called, but he definitely evened it up here. SURE DID! ::Legacy runs in and he and Marvelous stand looking around them, Joe Cool slowly gets to his feet. The three free agents stand in the ring, the three LOW members outside, looking in. The three free agents back up against each other and startle each other, they turn and face each other threateningly, ready to defend themselves.:: These free agents a little untrusting of each other it seems. They all want a contract, and they've got to be less organized than the LOW members. ::The LOW members patrol the outside of the ring, waiting for a chance to get in and strike, but the free agents, staying away from each other but guarding a part of the ring, don't let the LOW members in. The superstars fighting for the Aftermath title back off a bit, and start to wait for the two minute time to be up.:: Looks like the LOW guys are going to wait for another teammate! It's not a bad strategy. Right now its even in terms of numbers, its three on three. One team has the high ground, that's an advantage right there. the LOW members can't get in there without getting ripped apart as they try to get in, while they are defenseless. They need some back up, so they might as well wait a minute until they can possibly get some backup. But what if the final free agent comes out? Then they get hit from behind by one of them, and then they are pretty screwed. ::The LOW members wait it out, and soon enough the final buzzer rings, and the LOW members prepare to enter the ring, but Simon Krask runs out and tackles Jason Warrior. The other free agents jump out of the ring and a four on three assault starts. Chris Wrestling manages to break free and slide into the ring, where Legacy follows, as Simon Krask and Mike Marvelous take out Jason Warrior. Krask lifts Warrior up over his head and drops him down onto the guardrail, the wind getting knocked out of Warrior. Warrior lays on the ground gasping for breath as Krask grabs his leg, lifts it up, and kicks him in the back of the knee. Warrior slowly tries to crawl away but a hail of stomps from Krask and Marvelous rain down on him. Joe Cool whips Magnus into the guardrail, Magnus hits, comes off it and Joe Cool picks him up and whips him around into a powerslam.:: Right now it looks like the free agent team has the upper hand. Looks like it!? ::Jason Warrior gets up and Simon Krask grabs him around the back of the head and pulls his head down as he brings his knee up, smashing his knee into Warrior's face. Mean Mike follows it up with a few nudging kicks, until Trigger is backed into the corner. Though he can no longer control his movements, his fans plead to Meyhu to have mercy. Helpless, he is. Sitting there. Without the strength to keep his head up; without the lack of will to take a dive. Perhaps Meyhu is human after all. His tension melting away, he backs up and turns his back on the Infinite Warrior, to receive a standing ovation from the crowd. :: Good for him! Good for Meyhu, it seems as if he has finally learned how to treat others with the same respect-- :: Interrupting our heartwarming praise, a smirk comes over Meyhu's lips, and although it could be mistaken for pride at first, we soon see his intentions as he whips around and nails Trigger in the jaw with the most powerful kick yet. No, there's no mistake, as the temporary pop dies off into a rumbling chorus of boos. No, there's no mistake-- that look on Mike Meyhu's face is more definitely pride. :: Oh, that was low. That was lower than low, that was... That was dispicable. Mike Meyhu is dispicable. He's already got this match won, why won't he just stand back and let the referee count? I don't care about victories anymore. I don't care who owns LOW--I don't care if Trigger is even allowed to return. I just care about his well being. There might not be any Trigger left after this so-called match. Why, you ask? Why is Meyhu continuing to fight this after the battle has already been won? He's still fighting, because he waited twelve months to get Trigger one on one. He waited through mysteries, through scandals, through disasters... It is pure ecstacy for Meyhu every time his knuckles crack into Trigger's flesh. He wouldn't give it up for the world. :: Only now does Meyhu truly back off. Only now, now that the damage has been done, and he has lost the respect of the fans for the very last time. Never will there be trust for Mike Meyhu. Never should there have been. Looking now, it is so easy for Meyhu to earn their trust. Again and again they swear. And again and again he breaks all boundries. Now the real question comes. Will he end the match, or will he end it all? He doesn't have to make that choice. Another will. Because before he knows it, the fans are cheering again, and the Infinite Warrior is back on his feet. Second wind? Third wind? More than a dozen it seems now. More than a dozen career-enders, life-enders. And once again Trigger rises. But this time he will not settle for being brave. This time, he wants his revenge. With everything left in him, he runs towards his nemesis, and lets his emotions guide him. What will he do when he reaches the target? The target who still has his back turned on what could upset his plans for the last time. Trigger runs forth, and is only miliseconds away from reaching his goal. There is no need for panic now. There is no need for fear. He has it. Trigger has it. He can't be stopped. :: MY GOD! Trigger is really going for it! Trigger is going to take out Meyhu! So much for simply surviving, eh? :: There is no need for panic now. There is no need for fear. He has it now. Trigger has it. He can't be stopped. But then something unexpected happens. At the very last possible moment, Mean Mike Meyhu turns around, and when he sees the Infinite Warrior within range, he acts purely on instinct. He shoots out his arm, and adds momentum to Trigger's speed, sending him into a front somersault over the top rope, flying, flying, until he does a three-sixty and cracks his neck into the steel guardrail on the outside. A sickening thud rings out. So much for surviving. :: Fans: HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! HOLY SHIT! WE NEED TO GET SOME MEDICAL PERSONELL OUT HERE IMMEDIATELY! Holy shit... Holy shit!!! :: The mutual feeling throughout the arena is one that cannot be put into words. Fear, hatred, corruption, and respect all rolled into one. It cannot be put into words, but if it was, it would probably be a little something like this: holy shit. :: Oh my God! Oh my God! Somebody has to help Trigger here! I did not like the sound of the crash, nor do I like the awkward position in which his neck hit. I think the Infinite Warrior is seriously injured right now! No... This is too far... I never thought I'd hear myself saying this, but that was too extreme. Somebody needs to get out here and do something immediately... God... Holy shit... :: He just lies there, sprawled out next to the guardrail, in an arena full of fans watching his every move. More like his lack of movement. For the first time since Lights Out Wrestling hit the airwaves, there is complete silence. Nothing. Not a cough. Not a sneeze. Not a whisper. This is what it has all come down to. Life. Death. Mike Meyhu walks over to the side of the ring, and he gazes down at Trigger's corpse. He has no sympathy, he is not breaking character. There is no concern on his face, only curiousity. He wants to know the damage that he has done. :: Meyhu needs to get the hell back, because the spotlight isn't on him right now! He could have just murdered Trigger, and he doesn't care in the slightest. He's proud of what he's done. Can you believe that? He just keeps breaking down barriers, Meyhu does. He just keeps defying our tolerance. Now is where it needs to end. I'm... I'm... I'm speechless, Casey. I don't know what to say here. I just hope-- I just pray that Trigger will be alright. I just pray that Trigger will be alive. Please let him be alright. Please let him live. :: Again there is complete and utter silence, until the squeaking of a stretcher rolls down the rampway, accomponied by four medical workers dressed in blue uniforms. They are only doing their job as they carefully lift the Infinite Warrior onto the stretcher and strap him down, but they feal sick to their stomaches about it, and there is no denying. :: God, Jerry... You hate to see him go, but at the same time it's good that he'll be getting the best treatment money can buy. I just hope everything works out. It has to. :: And now, turning around the corner, they prepare to leave up the rampway, a public spectacle for all to see. Not the way Trigger would wish to go out, but not the way Meyhu would either. Mean Mike slides out of the squared circle when he realizes what is going on. He's not letting it end like this. :: Meyhu: TRIGGER! NO! :: He needs no microphone. The silence is defining. Meyhu easily catches up with the medics, and push them aside hard as they attempt to impede his actions. Unbuckling the straps, he slaps the helpless Warrior across the face, and yells into his hollow eyes. :: Meyhu: Trigger, you son of a bitch, wake up! We're not finished yet, Trigger. I'm gonna end this. Do you hear me? I'm gonna end this, not you!!! :: He goes out screaming at the body on the stretcher. They pull him away, and then they return to Trigger, preparing to move on with their duties. For twelve months, Trigger has fought for what he believed to be right. Trigger has fought against Mike Meyhu. And after twelve months, his battle has not yet ended. After twelve months, his battle will never end. Not unless he ends it now. Not unless he ends it forever. And with a noise ten times greater than that of a gunshot, Trigger's eyes open. The battle will end tonight. :: Oh my-- What? WHAT? Oh my God! :: He will not go down. Not without a fight. Gripping the sides of the stretcher, he rolls himself off the side, smacking into the floor. It doesn't matter. He will crawl to Meyhu if neccessary. The medics try to stop him, but he is a will determined to succeed. Magnificantly, he stands to his feet. His own two feet. His neck may be injured, his face may be bleeding, and his body may be throbbing with pain, but he is determined. He stumbles forward, and he needs no crutch. The fans remain silent, they need no words. Their adrenaline says a mouthful. Thousands upon thousands, their power beaming into the Warrior. They will be his senses. They will be his strength. :: He's... He's up! Trigger is up! Good lord, how the hell can this be possible? I thought he was dead! I thought he was gone--I thought he was truly injured! How is he still awake? How is he still alive? How is he still standing??? He may yet be gone. He may yet be injured. He is alive to serve that purpose, and nothing else matters until he finishes this unsettled business with Meyhu. After that, nothing will be the same. Throughout the coarse of history, there is a constant struggle between good and evil. It all comes down to this. What will the world be like, whether good prevails or crumbles to the forces of evil? What will our life be like? :: Mike Meyhu has already given up. He has already decided that whether it was by his own hands or not, he has defeated the Infinite Warrior. Infinite no longer. Or so he thinks. So he thinks, turning down the rampway, heading back to the ring, for some sort of celebration. It is no less a joyous day for the former World Champion... but not to the extent that it could be. Little does he know, it will be a joyous day after all. For one man. The anticipation is too much. By the time Trigger gets within reaching distance of his enemy, the fans cannot hold it in. They begin to cheer, and all at once the crowd is a constant hum of energy. Meyhu turns around in curiousity, a good act of fate for Trigger cannot turn him. What does he expect to see? The hand of the Warrior twitching slowly? Perhaps even a regaining of consciousness. What he does not expect to see is the Infinite Warrior directly in front of him, putting everything he has left into a weak kick to the midsection, and the heavy implant DDT known as the Warrior Realm. :: WARRIOR REALM! WARRIOR REALM! TRIGGER JUST HIT THE WARRIOR REALM! The excitement is through the roof in this building! Not only is Trigger alive and somewhat well, but he is making a comeback also! And this is not just any comeback-- this is a comeback destined to change the face of Lights Out Wrestling. Either he succeeds or he fails. He alone holds the power. He alone decides the fate of LOW. But if Meyhu's will is as strong as his, then what hope do we have? Meyhu's will is not as strong as Trigger's; Meyhu's will is not even close. There are certain benefits in the different ways that people live their lives. Mike Meyhu is evil and sadistic. Thus, he has the advantage of physical strength. But Trigger has lived his life good and pure, and the strength he has is on the inside. It will not be broken. Evil can never win. :: The reaction by the crowd is madening. The move itself does little damage to Meyhu's body, but to his ego it does devastation. To Trigger's pride it heals all wounds. Quickly the Most Powerful Man in the Business struggles to his feet. He is furious to be taken out with such a tactic. By somebody that by all means should be in a hospital, flatlining. By somebody who now, defies the odds again, and hits another Warrior Realm on Mean Mike Meyhu! The crash against the rampway echoes throughout the large building. Now, clutching his neck with one arm, Trigger raises the other triumphantly into the air, where he is met by a greeting of worship and respect. Now they chant for him, a chant that will hopefully foreshadow the future of the Warrior. :: Fans: IN-FIN-ITE! IN-FIN-IT-E! IN-FIN-IT-E! IN-FIN-IT-E! IN-FIN-ITE! IN-FIN-ITE! IN-FIN-ITE! IN-FIN-ITE! ANOTHER WARRIOR REALM! This is not just a comeback, Trigger is taking the advantage in this already long and brutal matchup. Yes, once again this is a matchup. Their struggles may be for more, but their futures reside here, in the wrestling arena, in the place where their troubles were born, and the place where these troubles will soon die. No, Jerry... It will never be a wrestling match. It never was. This is the seams that sew our lives together. Good versus evil. Good versus evil. It is almost too much. It is almost too little. It is everything. Nothing. There are no words to describe what this is, Jerry. But it is a struggle. An epic struggle. And we can only hope that it ends tonight. We can only hope that a resolution is on the horizen. :: Meyhu gets to his feet, but this time is filled with too much rage and humiliation to become victim of another Warrior Realm. Instead, he angrily shoves Trigger, who falls hard to the floor with ease. He is the Most Powerful Man in the Business, and he is putting everything he has into a wrestling match with quite possibly the weakest man in the world at the current moment. Trigger shouldn't even be alive. Mean Mike grabs Trigger by the collar and throws him fiercely into the ring, ripping his already-bloodsoaked tank top in the process. Trigger just lies there, gazing up at the ceiling, eyes rolling back into his head. He shouldn't have made it this far. But there's no going back. All there is now is the final confrontation. It will not be fought with weapons and fists, but with wits and courage. The mental battle will be the last. :: Trigger's comeback was great while it lasted, but now his adreniline has emptied out, and Meyhu has him right where he wants him: in the center of the ring without a place to run and without the strength to fight. He will to horrible things to the Infinite Warrior, I know he will. But it is something that needs to be done, something that needs to be over with. Now is the moment that we have all been waiting for, the moment that we have all been fearing. The final showdown. And I don't think that this showdown will deal with the fact that Trigger is nearly unconscious while Meyhu is barely breaking a sweat. It's going to deal with the very heart of their conflict. Trigger in his prime, Meyhu in his prime, fighting the battles of their lives. Fighting the battles for there lives. So that generations from now, the result will remain. There will be no second match. There will be no tie or draw. It ends here. It ends now. It ends for good. :: Meyhu slides in after him, and stands tall as the Infinite Warrior struggles to grasp for the ropes. He is no match for Meyhu's fury. Mean Mike grabs him by the head, and angrily turns him around. He lifts him to his feet, and then delivers a powerful kick to the gut. Trigger doubles over, where he is caught by Meyhu once again. Now, the Most Powerful Man in the Business lifts him up over his head, and rests him on his broad shoulders. Trigger is defenseless. Trigger is helpless. He knows what is coming next. We all do. :: Oh my God! Mike Meyhu is about to hit the Meyhu Meltdown on Trigger, who in my eyes has already been defeated! This will be disastor... Somebody has to do something before Meyhu goes too far tonight! He's already gone too far tonight. Trigger has no chance of escaping from the Meyhu Meltdown... His only hope is that a miracle will come, and save him from this web in which he has become entagled in. His only hope is that somebody hears his plea, and somebody decides to make a difference. The tension is mount. Good versus evil. Good versus evil. Which will prevail? Which will crumble to a fiery demise? Moments away. Just moments away. In just moments, we will all know the answer. In just moments, it will all be so clear. Twelve months in the making. It will all be so clear. No, Casey, not just twelve months. Years. Decades. Centuries. Eternities. This is a struggle that was started before time began, but will end here. Right now. Moments away. :: Meyhu slowly turns in a narrow circle. He smirks into the endless maze of faces, booing and jeering. But their words have no impact now. Not where Meyhu has his foe. Not where Meyhu has the Infinite Warrior. The match has been bloody, there is no doubt. But eternal struggle or not, all wrestling matches must come to an end. And they all come to an end with a wrestling move. On his shoulders, Trigger has no hope of escape. All he can hope for is a miracle. A miracle... Meyhu turns his body all at once, plowing Trigger head-first into the mat with a force only matched with the response from the raging fans. For every action there is a reaction. A miracle... No escape. No miracle. No chance of survival. We prayed for it to end a different way, but it was destined from the beginning. Evil will sworm us like locus, engulfing this company and eventually all of us into its murky fog. There is no escape now. There is no reversing the decision, there is no hope of a miracle. But we mustn't give away our hope, because without hope, we truly are defenseless. Without hope, we truly are nothing. :: The shock of the finishing manuver thunders throughout the arena. There is no need for anxiety anymore. 13... 14... 15... Is that a chuckle we hear, coming from the lips of the former World Champion? 16... 17... 18... Yes. Tristan Belmont just hit the Battle Cry through our announcing table, onto Mean Mike Meyhu. I don't mind almost being crushed. I wouldn't mind being crushed. It was for a good cause-- A damn good cause. The fans are estatic here tonight, and frankly I am as well. However much strength Mike Meyhu had before has been erased now. However much evil pumped through his veins has been silenced now. He can't run. He can't hide. This is what it feels like to have a world at peace. :: Stepping back into the debris, Tristan pulls Meyhu from the wreckage, and accomponies him into the ring. Even though Trigger, the Warrior who initiated this battle, is unconscious with his eyes rolling back into his head, the battle is coming to a close, and his presence is enough. 19... 20... 21... 22... 23... 24... 25... 26... 27... Now is where it counts. Nothing has mattered until now. Millions of eyes stare and see the same sight which we see now. Complete silences fills the building once again. The referee's hand slapping against the mat are more gunshots ringing out. 28. 29. 30. And the war has now come to a close. :: IT'S OVER! IT'S OVER! TRIGGER HAS DEFEATED MIKE MEYHU! TRIGGER IS ONCE AGAIN OWNER OF LIGHTS OUT WRESTLING! WE WILL NEVER HAVE TO SEE MIKE MEYHU'S FACE AGAIN! This is a happy day for LOW, and a happy day for the world as we know it. And Jerry, though the victory has only just taken place, I already feel a different emotion in the air. I know what you're talking about, Casey; I feel it too. :: The bell rings, and Meyhu is helped to his feet by the referee. He does not need to be pointed in the direction of the exit. There is a breif moment of silence... It comemorates the one year of hard work and effort, along with all the accomplishments of Mike Meyhu. It is a better thing that he is now gone. And as he disappears behind the curtain, he knows that he will not be forgotten. :: It's almost sad to see him go, but when you think of how much better off LOW will be, there is no hesitation. It's a very good thing. :: And as soon as he has officially left, officially gone for good, the true celebration begins. Tristan, with a gleaming smile, grabs a microphone and attempts to speak over the roaring of the crowd. Trigger is only half-conscious, however... His eyes flicker as he tries to take note of the situation. :: Belmont: We did it, Trigger! You and me, we got rid of Meyhu! :: He hands the microphone over to the Infinite Warrior, who still cannot speak. :: Belmont: He's gone, Trigger, we got rid of him! :: Confused, Trigger tries to speak. :: Trigger: We? :: The insane pop does not die down-- thousands of freed souls cannot be silenced. :: Belmont: Yes, Trigger. You and me both. I hit him with the Battle Cry! :: Remeniscent joy now... The reaction could not be any greater. :: He sure as hell did! :: Oddly enough, Trigger's face is not filled with excitement. Nor glee. Nor the least bit of happiness. He stares sternly towards his student, as if he cannot comprehend what he has heard. :: Trigger: You mean... I didn't defeat him on my own? :: Only now does it occur to the Demonic Warrior that he may have committed a fault. :: Belmont: Well... :: He needs not to explain. The footage of the previous moments rolls across the tron, and a fully aware Trigger sees first-hand the actions that led towards Mike Meyhu's fall from power. :: Ahh, it feels even better to watch it a second time. :: But Trigger is still not enthusiastic. In fact, he looks almost paranoid, with sweat once again forming across his forehead and his body shaking violently. :: Trigger: I-I-I didn't do it on my own? :: Tristan tries to comfort him. :: Belmont: It's okay... All that matters is that he's gone. Now there is nothing else to fear. :: Violently, the Infinite Warrior pushes him away, and the fans do not know what to think. :: Trigger: No, no, it's not okay! This was my battle, this was my battle! I wanted to end it. I needed to end it! Everything in my life lead up to this confrontation. I didn't care who won. All that mattered was that it was over. I WANT IT TO BE OVER, TRISTAN! I WANT IT TO BE OVER! :: He drops the microphone with a loud crash on the mat, and breaks down into tears. Tristan's eyes go wide. :: Belmont: I'm sorry, Trigger... I didn't know... :: It does not stop his intense sobbing. :: Belmont: What can I do to make this right? :: Biting his lip, the Infinite Warrior wipes his eyes and glances solemnly towards Tristan's face. :: Trigger: There's nothing you can do. I need to go. :: With that, he pushes past Tristan and heads for the ropes. :: Belmont: Wait! Trigger! Where are you going? :: Teary-eyed once more, he steps through the middle ropes and jumps off of the apron. :: Trigger: I need to go. I need to leave, and I must never return. :: Silence sweeps over the crowd, a chain reaction like the atomic bomb. :: Trigger: All I ever wanted was this one thing. All I ever wanted was closure. :: He turns to go, exiting through the entranceway, pulling the show to a close, but not before he utters his final words. :: Trigger: I need to leave, Tristan, and I must never return.
